 <body>
  <a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050"></a><a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050"></a>
  <h1 class="topictitle1">Ascend C API列表</h1>
  <div id="body0000001917094304">
   <div class="p" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__zh-cn_topic_0000001762058545_zh-cn_topic_0000001589265601_p131911195110">
    <span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__zh-cn_topic_0000001762058545_zh-cn_topic_0000001589265601_ph10546143515149">Ascend C</span>提供一组类库API，开发者使用标准C++语法和类库API进行编程。<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__zh-cn_topic_0000001762058545_ph19399631793">Ascend C</span>编程类库API示意图如下所示，分为：
    <ul id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ul178033061311">
     <li id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__li16122723171312"><strong id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b36519107343">基础数据结构</strong>：kernel API中使用到的基础数据结构，比如GlobalTensor和LocalTensor。</li>
     <li id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__li16880164615139"><strong id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__zh-cn_topic_0000002484241782_b02010568362">语言扩展层 C API</strong>：开放芯片完备编程能力，支持数组分配内存，一般基于指针编程，提供与业界一致的C语言编程体验。</li>
     <li id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__li58329249132"><strong id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b3733171263416">基础API</strong>：实现对硬件能力的抽象，开放芯片的能力，保证完备性和兼容性。标注为ISASI（Instruction Set Architecture Special Interface，硬件体系结构相关的接口）类别的API，不能保证跨硬件版本兼容。</li>
     <li id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__li7347152641316"><strong id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b1049991519343">高阶API</strong>：实现一些常用的计算算法，用于提高编程开发效率，通常会调用多种基础API实现。高阶API包括数学库、Matmul、Softmax等API。高阶API可以保证兼容性。</li>
     <li id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__li15963594289"><strong id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b157364512321">SIMT API</strong>：单指令多线程API。以单条指令多个线程的形式来实现并行计算。SIMT编程主要用于向量计算，特别适合处理离散访问、复杂控制逻辑等场景。</li>
     <li id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__li67671127219"><strong id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__b69031024152113">Utils API（公共辅助函数）</strong>：丰富的通用工具类，涵盖标准库、平台信息获取、运行时编译及日志输出等功能，支持开发者高效实现算子开发与性能优化。</li>
    </ul>
   </div>
   <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6410155335011"></p>
   <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p162780549507"><span><img class="eddx" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__image43427554509" src="figure/zh-cn_image_0000002562303741.png"></span></p>
   <div class="section" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__section3119442205518">
    <h4 class="sectiontitle">基础数据结构</h4>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table16268175715515" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表1 </b>基础数据结构列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11268657105518">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" id="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1026810577553">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" id="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p226835735518">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row102681557105520">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12251181311560"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0006.html">LocalTensor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2025215484579"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph4396131516585">LocalTensor用于存放AI Core中Local Memory（内部存储）的数据，支持逻辑位置<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0174.html">TPosition</a>为VECIN、VECOUT、VECCALC、A1、A2、B1、B2、CO1、CO2。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row026925718557">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p142691057115518"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0007.html">GlobalTensor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p625254835716"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph698815359590">GlobalTensor用来存放Global Memory（外部存储）的全局数据。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row32691573557">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8269195705514"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00143.html">Coordinate</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1825015488573">Coordinate本质上是一个元组（tuple），用于表示张量在不同维度的位置信息，即坐标值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row926915745510">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p192691757185519"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00076.html">Layout</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1724954818576"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph198761643125913">Layout&lt;Shape, Stride&gt;数据结构是描述多维张量内存布局的基础模板类，通过编译时的形状（Shape）和步长（Stride）信息，实现逻辑坐标空间到一维内存地址空间的映射，为复杂张量操作和硬件优化提供基础支持。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row97101411165713">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5710711115712"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0011.html">TensorTrait</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.4.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p167101111155717"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph287618459597">TensorTrait数据结构是描述Tensor相关信息的基础模板类，包含Tensor的数据类型、逻辑位置和Layout内存布局。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
   </div>
   <div class="section" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__section117632211201">
    <h4 class="sectiontitle">基础API</h4>
    <div class="tablenoborder">

      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row69936217246">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" id="mcps1.3.5.2.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1799422162414">接口名</p></th>

         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p89941221202417">功能描述</p></th>

      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19994142132410">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p119488415242"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0101.html">DataCopy</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1188623312246">数据搬运接口，包括普通数据搬运、增强数据搬运、切片数据搬运、随路格式转换。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row29942216241">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p138851338243"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0106.html">Copy</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1488517337242">VECIN、VECCALC、VECOUT之间的搬运指令，支持mask操作和<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1256166185416">DataBlock</span>间隔操作。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">

       </tr>

       </tr>

       </tr>

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p389445174112">按元素求最小值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row14127182319571">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1448115232"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0054.html">Adds</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p884518405416">矢量内每个元素与标量求和。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row107601527165713">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p44471122316"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0055.html">Muls</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p577119572225">矢量内每个元素与标量求积。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1277082912579">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8441215238"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0056.html">Maxs</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18672613113517">源操作数矢量内每个元素与标量相比，如果比标量大，则取源操作数值，比标量的值小，则取标量值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row33851439175710">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9441515231"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0057.html">Mins</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18276162693419">源操作数矢量内每个元素与标量相比，如果比标量大，则取标量值，比标量的值小，则取源操作数值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row547912419575">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5445162310"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0060.html">LeakyRelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p27096582222">按元素做带泄露线性整流Leaky ReLU。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11611570274">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="2" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p570714385282"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00018.html">Subs</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1616145717273"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph452415255306">矢量内每个元素和标量间做减法，支持标量在前和标量在后两种场景，其中标量输入支持配置LocalTensor单点元素。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13124593270">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1779354482813"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00019.html">Divs</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p191212595270"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph58341357153014">矢量内每个元素和标量间做除法，支持标量在前和标量在后两种场景，其中标量输入支持配置LocalTensor单点元素。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row64233134354">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="5" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p771519716593">逻辑计算</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p24122327439"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0031.html">Not</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1694421944312">按元素做按位取反。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row855712515365">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p512110487389"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0041.html">And</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p555732515368">针对每对元素执行按位与运算。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19695152573617">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12122548183814"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0042.html">Or</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17348124922015">针对每对元素执行按位或运算。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row20711575584">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p644313232"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0058.html">ShiftLeft</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1676331375210">对源操作数中的每个元素进行左移操作，左移的位数由输入参数scalarValue决定。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row17751175445811">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9447119234"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0059.html">ShiftRight</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p97121651191211">对源操作数中的每个元素进行右移操作，右移的位数由输入参数scalarValue决定。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1739723113211">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="2" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1239733112327">逻辑计算</p>
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p76622335323"></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1691819570327"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00020.html">Ands</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3397531133218"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph107741166344">矢量内每个元素和标量间做与操作，支持标量在前和标量在后两种场景，其中标量输入支持配置LocalTensor单点元素。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2662163318324">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12888184123313"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00021.html">Ors</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p566393373216"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph11500142416349">矢量内每个元素和标量间做或操作，支持标量在前和标量在后两种场景，其中标量输入支持配置LocalTensor单点元素。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1413722319810">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="11" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12137102320816">复合计算</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1885912584229"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0063.html">Axpy</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1485935816222">源操作数中每个元素与标量求积后和目的操作数中的对应元素相加。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row167541318782">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3734133111451"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0074.html">CastDequant</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1924211214434">对输入做量化并进行精度转换。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15953122533617">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19122184810389"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0043.html">AddRelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p39535252363">按元素求和，结果和0对比取较大值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row16141192653616">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p112214815384"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0044.html">AddReluCast</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p514172623613">按元素求和，结果和0对比取较大值，并根据源操作数和目的操作数Tensor的数据类型进行精度转换。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row977016176378">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1312214873810"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0045.html">AddDeqRelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1477071717372">依次计算按元素求和、结果进行deq量化后再进行relu计算（结果和0对比取较大值）。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row17935161713713">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1012244843817"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0046.html">SubRelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p149363171378">按元素求差，结果和0对比取较大值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row138841816370">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p51221848173810"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0047.html">SubReluCast</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p158818183378">按元素求差，结果和0对比取较大值，并根据源操作数和目的操作数Tensor的数据类型进行精度转换。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row132241218193712">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p212217485389"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0048.html">MulAddDst</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5224201818378">按元素将src0Local和src1Local相乘并和dstLocal相加，将最终结果存放进dstLocal中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row117041522154616">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19705182219464"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0049.html">MulCast</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12705182217466">按元素求积，并根据源操作数和目的操作数Tensor的数据类型进行精度转换。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row193701818163720">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p91222048133815"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0050.html">FusedMulAdd</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1037019189371">按元素将src0Local和dstLocal相乘并加上src1Local，最终结果存放入dstLocal。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1075883983914">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19675842114214"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0051.html">MulAddRelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p475993916396">按元素将src0Local和dstLocal相乘并加上src1Local，将结果和0作比较，取较大值，最终结果存放进dstLocal中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row187959172211">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="5" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p147155915229">比较与选择</p>
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19980131874015"></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p157105910222"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0066.html">Compare</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1745910221">逐元素比较两个tensor大小，如果比较后的结果为真，则输出结果的对应比特位为1，否则为0。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1314575919229">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1314535915221"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0067.html">Compare（结果存放入寄存器）</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p177959415503">逐元素比较两个tensor大小，如果比较后的结果为真，则输出结果的对应比特位为1，否则为0。Compare接口需要mask参数时，可以使用此接口。计算结果存放入寄存器中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row31514186404">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7151171817406"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0068.html">Compares</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7227113135415">逐元素比较一个tensor中的元素和另一个Scalar的大小，如果比较后的结果为真，则输出结果的对应比特位为1，否则为0。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1798081810408">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p61901953124213"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0070.html">Select</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p298081819406">给定两个源操作数src0和src1，根据selMask（用于选择的Mask掩码）的比特位值选取元素，得到目的操作数dst。选择的规则为：当selMask的比特位是1时，从src0中选取，比特位是0时从src1选取。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1214111974012">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1719055394219"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0071.html">GatherMask</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p182166215152">以内置固定模式对应的二进制或者用户自定义输入的Tensor数值对应的二进制为gather mask（数据收集的掩码），从源操作数中选取元素写入目的操作数中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1095112164319">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1357761654319">精度转换指令</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p757710162439"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0073.html">Cast</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5969213430">根据源操作数和目的操作数Tensor的数据类型进行精度转换。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1651510211434">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="11" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1757701610436">归约计算</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1357731618438"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0076.html">ReduceMax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1351515264314">在所有的输入数据中找出最大值及最大值对应的索引位置。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row196635294315">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p457871613433"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0077.html">ReduceMin</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6853520201916">在所有的输入数据中找出最小值及最小值对应的索引位置。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row77918244313">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p35781716164313"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0078.html">ReduceSum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1546312559583">对所有的输入数据求和。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row79421217439">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p65788161435"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0079.html">WholeReduceMax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p193962509594">每个repeat内所有数据求最大值以及其索引index。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1961802720599">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p961972710592"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0080.html">WholeReduceMin</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0619192712592">每个repeat内所有数据求最小值以及其索引index。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2928152965915">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1492892975917"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0081.html">WholeReduceSum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p199283290594">每个repeat内所有数据求和。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row157712324319">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3578151614430"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0082.html">BlockReduceMax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p177391045819">对每个repeat内所有元素求最大值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row17211133134315">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1057841619435"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0083.html">BlockReduceMin</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2021183144310">对每个repeat内所有元素求最小值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row193401338432">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1557814168438"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0084.html">BlockReduceSum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p998114445293">对每个repeat内所有元素求和。源操作数相加采用二叉树方式，两两相加。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row250213184316">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p957811615437"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0085.html">PairReduceSum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6502183184316">PairReduceSum：相邻两个（奇偶）元素求和。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5812182116350">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p198129212351"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0086.html">RepeatReduceSum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1481215214350">每个repeat内所有数据求和。和<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0081.html">WholeReduceSum</a>接口相比，不支持mask逐bit模式。建议使用功能更全面的<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0081.html">WholeReduceSum</a>接口。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row109261892541">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="2" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p629093119547">数据转换</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p42901731145414"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0199.html">Transpose</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1429010318547">可实现16*16的二维矩阵数据块的转置和[N,C,H,W]与[N,H,W,C]互相转换。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row476616718548">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p629033110540"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0200.html">TransDataTo5HD</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p162901631125410">数据格式转换，一般用于将NCHW格式转换成NC1HWC0格式。特别的，也可以用于二维矩阵数据块的转置。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row86261631104316">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="3" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p203231339194317">数据填充</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p432393920436"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0088.html">Duplicate</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9626431124314">将一个变量或一个立即数，复制多次并填充到向量。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row17641318431">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p232383912436"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0089.html">Brcb</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p911mcpsimp">给定一个输入张量，每一次取输入张量中的8个数填充到结果张量的8个datablock（32Bytes）中去，每个数对应一个datablock。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row173409456360">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p24100384369"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0090.html">CreateVecIndex</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p234064518368">以firstValue为起始值创建向量索引。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row161901532124317">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2323163915431">数据分散/数据收集</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1232318396431"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0092.html">Gather</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p161901632134314">给定输入的张量和一个地址偏移张量，Gather指令根据偏移地址将输入张量按元素收集到结果张量中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row16331932164312">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="4" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1632373914312">掩码操作</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p43241739154316"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0094.html">SetMaskCount</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9633133216430">设置mask模式为Counter模式。该模式下，不需要开发者去感知迭代次数、处理非对齐的尾块等操作，可直接传入计算数据量，实际迭代次数由Vector计算单元自动推断。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1678513323437">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15324339114312"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0095.html">SetMaskNorm</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1785143218431">设置mask模式为Normal模式。该模式为系统默认模式，支持开发者配置迭代次数。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1393713216436">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p23247397438"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0096.html">SetVectorMask</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p993873224313">用于在矢量计算时设置mask。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15756339433">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3324039144317"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0097.html">ResetMask</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p57533324312">恢复mask的值为默认值（全1），表示矢量计算中每次迭代内的所有元素都将参与运算。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row743516194407">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="15.590000000000002%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p23240390438">量化设置</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.64%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10324173918436"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0099.html">SetDeqScale</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.77%" headers="mcps1.3.5.3.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1238122924318">设置DEQSCALE寄存器的值。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table339023582010" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表4 </b>标量计算API列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1539063572010">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" id="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13390235192015">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" id="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2390103519209">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row839013512016">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2023773313396"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0016.html">GetBitCount</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1739063514204">获取一个uint64_t类型数字的二进制中0或者1的个数。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5390935132010">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9743558403"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0017.html">CountLeadingZero</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p739014352201">计算一个uint64_t类型数字前导0的个数（二进制从最高位到第一个1一共有多少个0）。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row187241145152620">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19725445132613"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0018.html">Cast（float转half、int32_t）</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p772515454262">将一个scalar的类型转换为指定的类型。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row129095914341">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15290059143412"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0019.html">CountBitsCntSameAsSignBit</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p05116051416">计算一个uint64_t类型数字的二进制中，从最高数值位开始与符号位相同的连续比特位的个数。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row944019598346">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1062632319354"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0020.html">GetSFFValue</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13440259173413">获取一个uint64_t类型数字的二进制中第一个0或1出现的位置。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1566175913418">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9566659173420"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0021.html">Cast（float转bfloat16_t）</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p828219133366">float类型标量数据转换成bfloat16_t类型标量数据。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13704105910340">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16704195953414"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0022.html">Cast（多类型转float）</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15293151211360">bfloat16_t类型标量数据转换成float类型标量数据。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table1267664316264" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表5 </b>资源管理API列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15676154310267">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" id="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6676543192617">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" id="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p146761434266">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row367664312619">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p667654312618"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0108.html">TPipe</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6428345172717">TPipe是用来管理全局内存等资源的框架。通过TPipe类提供的接口可以完成内存等资源的分配管理操作。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1867604312262">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p616062319285"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0120.html">GetTPipePtr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p26771543172611">获取框架当前管理全局内存的TPipe指针，用户获取指针后，可进行TPipe相关的操作。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1381344122816">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p681124418285"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0121.html">TBufPool</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1062105494410">TPipe可以管理全局内存资源，而TBufPool可以手动管理或复用<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1088254310583">Unified Buffer</span>/<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1535518221316">L1 Buffer</span>物理内存，主要用于多个stage计算中<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph791414174415">Unified Buffer</span>/<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph7621654184416">L1 Buffer</span>物理内存不足的场景。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1032714367308">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p73271836193011"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0136.html">TQue</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13327163673017">提供入队出队等接口，通过队列（Queue）完成任务间同步。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row6569103614302">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p165691368309"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0146.html">TQueBind</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9569203683010">TQueBind绑定源逻辑位置和目的逻辑位置，根据源位置和目的位置，来确定内存分配的位置 、插入对应的同步事件，帮助开发者解决内存分配和管理、同步等问题。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row106614368306">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1866114362309"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0160.html">TBuf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12661203618308">使用<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph438819594207">Ascend C</span>编程的过程中，可能会用到一些临时变量。这些临时变量占用的内存可以使用TBuf数据结构来管理。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row158561936163012">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p545354010463"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0166.html">InitSpmBuffer</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16856103663012">初始化SPM Buffer。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row324173716309">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1245314074616"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0167.html">WriteSpmBuffer</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17575330684">将需要溢出暂存的数据拷贝到SPM Buffer中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1515119377302">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19453154018462"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0168.html">ReadSpmBuffer</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1881419176258">从SPM Buffer读回到local数据中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1550403614616">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4453154014462"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0172.html">GetUserWorkspace</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19504103614618">获取用户使用的workspace指针。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15918536184616">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p54539406461"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0171.html">SetSysWorkSpace</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4918163654611">在进行融合算子编程时，由于框架通信机制需要使用到workspace，也就是系统workspace，所以在该场景下，开发者要调用该接口，设置系统workspace的指针。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row196011374465">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2045304004615"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0170.html">GetSysWorkSpacePtr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p86018377467">获取系统workspace指针。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table921112251162" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表6 </b>同步控制API列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row975619311161">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" id="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8307249121617">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" id="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0308184961620">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1021262515169">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p62931833203117"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0178.html">TQueSync</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p82931433203116">TQueSync类提供同步控制接口，开发者可以使用这类API来自行完成同步控制。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1821272513168">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1190314213216"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0202.html">IBSet</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15692228145311">当不同核之间操作同一块全局内存且可能存在读后写、写后读以及写后写等数据依赖问题时，通过调用该函数来插入同步语句来避免上述数据依赖时可能出现的数据读写错误问题。调用IBSet设置某一个核的标志位，与IBWait成对出现配合使用，表示核之间的同步等待指令，等待某一个核操作完成。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row121219258161">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13903164214324"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0203.html">IBWait</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p27141049175319">当不同核之间操作同一块全局内存且可能存在读后写、写后读以及写后写等数据依赖问题时，通过调用该函数来插入同步语句来避免上述数据依赖时可能出现的数据读写错误问题。IBWait与IBSet成对出现配合使用，表示核之间的同步等待指令，等待某一个核操作完成。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1121220254168">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8904942143216"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0204.html">SyncAll</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12782512105416">当不同核之间操作同一块全局内存且可能存在读后写、写后读以及写后写等数据依赖问题时，通过调用该函数来插入同步语句来避免上述数据依赖时可能出现的数据读写错误问题。目前多核同步分为硬同步和软同步，硬件同步是利用硬件自带的全核同步指令由硬件保证多核同步，软件同步是使用软件算法模拟实现。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row92121225191611">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p29094917345"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0206.html">InitDetermineComputeWorkspace</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9909129113414">初始化GM共享内存的值，完成初始化后才可以调用<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0207.html">WaitPreBlock</a>和<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0208.html">NotifyNextBlock</a>。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row16213425171615">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p390910913341"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0207.html">WaitPreBlock</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p49091792347">通过读GM地址中的值，确认是否需要继续等待，当GM的值满足当前核的等待条件时，该核即可往下执行，进行下一步操作。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row152131825131610">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p29091594341"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0208.html">NotifyNextBlock</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6909899349">通过写GM地址，通知下一个核当前核的操作已完成，下一个核可以进行操作。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row52131925101618">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p39841259102515"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00087.html">SetNextTaskStart</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12934317236"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1882043515233">在SuperKernel的子Kernel中调用，调用后的指令可以和后续其他的子Kernel实现并行，提升整体性能。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row162135257162">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p288919718266"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00088.html">WaitPreTaskEnd</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p632496172313"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1925517378244">在SuperKernel的子Kernel中调用，调用前的指令可以和前序其他的子Kernel实现并行，提升整体性能。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table5254131810573" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表7 </b>缓存处理API列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row32541018135711">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.27%" id="mcps1.3.5.7.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17682103125810">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.730000000000004%" id="mcps1.3.5.7.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p26828319585">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1325501835712">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.27%" headers="mcps1.3.5.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p83441336473"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0176.html">DataCachePreload</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.730000000000004%" headers="mcps1.3.5.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18111100154719">从源地址所在的特定DDR地址预加载数据到data cache中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5255181815720">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.27%" headers="mcps1.3.5.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1134473164714"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0177.html">DataCacheCleanAndInvalid</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.730000000000004%" headers="mcps1.3.5.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p647301034415">该接口用来刷新Cache，保证Cache的一致性。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table26716458301" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表8 </b>系统变量访问API列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15672134518304">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" id="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18672144511306">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" id="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p56726451306">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row967212454304">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1194732015475"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0184.html">GetBlockNum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p585381495015">获取当前任务配置的Block数，用于代码内部的多核逻辑控制等。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1967224513011">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p11947620104710"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0185.html">GetBlockIdx</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p54921825165017">获取当前core的index，用于代码内部的多核逻辑控制及多核偏移量计算等。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11672104593014">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17947182012472"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0186.html">GetDataBlockSizeInBytes</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14250113816503">获取当前芯片版本一个datablock的大小，单位为byte。开发者根据datablock的大小来计算API指令中待传入的repeatTime 、<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph06845043917">DataBlock Stride</span>、<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph13946527113916">Repeat Stride</span>等参数值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row3672445153011">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1594715207476"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0187.html">GetArchVersion</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p764334516501">获取当前AI处理器架构版本号。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row4284193416298">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.37%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12284163417298"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00094.html">InitSocState</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1828453452913">由于AI Core上存在一些全局状态，如原子累加状态、Mask模式等，在实际运行中，这些值可以被前序执行的算子修改而导致计算出现不符合预期的行为，在<a href="/document/detail/zh/CANNCommunityEdition/900/programug/Ascendcopdevg/atlas_ascendc_10_00019.html">静态Tensor编程</a>的场景中用户必须在Kernel入口处调用此函数来初始化AI Core状态 。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table1395854383210" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表9 </b>原子操作接口列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row12958043173211">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" id="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2958104363212">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" id="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0958174353211">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1077235835018">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10729145920507"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0210.html">SetAtomicAdd</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20143125311516">设置接下来从VECOUT到GM，L0C到GM，L1到GM的数据传输是否进行原子累加，可根据参数不同设定不同的累加数据类型。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row139588436327">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1772985912505"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0211.html">SetAtomicType</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p38701310125517">通过设置模板参数来设定原子操作不同的数据类型。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row595874313218">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1870510588519"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0212.html">DisableDmaAtomic</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1214271020615">原子操作函数，清空原子操作的状态。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2084965011239">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1359015148246"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00259.html">AtomicAdd</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p108491650102316"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1305389278">调用该接口后，可在指定GM地址上进行原子加操作。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row171030533239">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p324502616243"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00260.html">AtomicMin</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18103053142317"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph169593419276">调用该接口后，可在指定GM地址上进行原子比较取小操作。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1836819554234">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p142593217246"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00261.html">AtomicMax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17368655132317"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph192894512276">调用该接口后，可在指定GM地址上进行原子取大操作。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row437810019249">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p143501939152411"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00262.html">AtomicCas</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1637810162415"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1570715132720">调用该接口后，可在指定GM地址上进行原子比较，如果和value1相等，则把value2的值赋值到GM上；如果和value1不相等，则GM上的值不变。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1223315282416">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p11984124519248"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00263.html">AtomicExch</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p123311213245"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph8490145722711">在GM内存中执行原子交换操作。具体来说，它读取指定GM地址上的数据，并将新的值存储回同一地址。函数返回旧值。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table18295429109" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表10 </b>调试接口列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row530154201012">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" id="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p113014281016">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" id="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1230154221011">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row4220222125515">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p741723545516"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0192.html">DumpTensor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1541723514559">基于算子工程开发的算子，可以使用该接口Dump指定Tensor的内容。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row153018215552">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p144177356555"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0193.html">printf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1941711350553">基于算子工程开发的算子，可以使用该接口实现CPU侧/NPU侧调试场景下的格式化输出功能。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row18839203119378">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17828375379"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00168.html">ascendc_assert</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p138203733710"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph5884122310382">ascendc_assert提供了一种在CPU/NPU域实现断言功能的接口。当断言条件不满足时，系统会输出断言信息并格式化打印在屏幕上。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row8256721175511">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p34186352551"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0194.html">assert</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1141883535519">基于算子工程开发的算子，可以使用该接口实现CPU/NPU域assert断言功能。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row820972155519">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0418143555511"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0195.html">DumpAccChkPoint</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9418735175516">基于算子工程开发的算子，可以使用该接口Dump指定Tensor的内容。该接口可以支持指定偏移位置的Tensor打印。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15977214153717">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1068951853720"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00002.html">PrintTimeStamp</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p149786149371"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph18761185413715">提供时间戳打点功能，用于在算子Kernel代码中标记关键执行点。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row14208102016559">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12418123512551"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0196.html">Trap</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1941853512557">当软件产生异常后，使用该指令使kernel中止运行。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row830164241016">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13406234125916"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1207.html">GmAlloc</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__zh-cn_topic_0000001963639306_zh-cn_topic_0000001541764188_p1531015912615">进行核函数的CPU侧运行验证时，用于创建共享内存：在/tmp目录下创建一个共享文件，并返回该文件的映射指针。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row88415865818">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p161386446595"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1208.html">ICPU_RUN_KF</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6851188585">进行核函数的CPU侧运行验证时，CPU调测总入口，完成CPU侧的算子程序调用。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row7702750145910">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19702850185918"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1209.html">ICPU_SET_TILING_KEY</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10702750175919">用于指定本次CPU调测使用的tilingKey。调测执行时，将只执行算子核函数中该tilingKey对应的分支。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row10114145135910">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p31141451185911"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1210.html">GmFree</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p311495195915">进行核函数的CPU侧运行验证时，用于释放通过GmAlloc申请的共享内存。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1125615511592">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16416351306"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1211.html">SetKernelMode</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__zh-cn_topic_0000001963639310_zh-cn_topic_0000001656094169_p1118165416116">CPU调测时，设置内核模式为单AIV模式，单AIC模式或者MIX模式，以分别支持单AIV矢量算子，单AIC矩阵算子，MIX混合算子的CPU调试。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5399851115914">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18391515913"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1212.html">TRACE_START</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p133991451205919">通过CAModel进行算子性能仿真时，可对算子任意运行阶段打点，从而分析不同指令的流水图，以便进一步性能调优。</p>
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3807143716119">用于表示起始位置打点，一般与<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1213.html">TRACE_STOP</a>配套使用。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row125261851195913">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p66051550125"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1213.html">TRACE_STOP</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__zh-cn_topic_0000002000199857_p15636113710169">通过CAModel进行算子性能仿真时，可对算子任意运行阶段打点，从而分析不同指令的流水图，以便进一步性能调优。</p>
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__zh-cn_topic_0000002000199857_p868234414164">用于表示终止位置打点，一般与<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1212.html">TRACE_START</a>配套使用。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row368519581124">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1468611581226"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1214.html">MetricsProfStart</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16869582024">用于设置性能数据采集信号启动，和MetricsProfStop配合使用。使用<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph766254331714"><a href="https://www.hiascend.com/document/detail/zh/CANNCommunityEdition/900/devaids/optool/docs/zh/user_guide/msopprof_user_guide.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">算子调优（msProf）</a></span>工具进行算子上板调优时，可在kernel侧代码段前后分别调用MetricsProfStart和MetricsProfStop来指定需要调优的代码段范围。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2143959125">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4507261733"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1215.html">MetricsProfStop</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.5.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__zh-cn_topic_0000002000280001_zh-cn_topic_0000001960477980_p279125418620">设置性能数据采集信号停止，和MetricsProfStart配合使用。使用<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph735171605518"><a href="https://www.hiascend.com/document/detail/zh/CANNCommunityEdition/900/devaids/optool/docs/zh/user_guide/msopprof_user_guide.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">算子调优（msProf）</a></span>工具进行算子上板调优时，可在kernel侧代码段前后分别调用MetricsProfStart和MetricsProfStop来指定需要调优的代码段范围。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table9496143191816" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表11 </b>工具函数接口列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row14962043181812">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" id="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p449784310180">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" id="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17497204317180">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row649710437184">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p149744319183"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00163.html">Async</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p816712203211"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph585624314195">Async提供了一个统一的接口，用于在不同模式下（AIC或AIV）执行特定函数，从而避免代码中直接的硬件条件判断（如使用ASCEND_IS_AIV或ASCEND_IS_AIC）。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2010610593211">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p317115102325"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00045.html">NumericLimits</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17106857321"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1575202123315">NumericLimits工具类，用于查询指定数据类型的最大值/最小值等属性。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row154197811816">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2094712054713"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0188.html">GetTaskRatio</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p29729544501">适用于Cube/Vector分离模式，用来获取Cube/Vector的配比。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row12385124516283">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17516125692817"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0189.html">GetUBSizeInBytes</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17516165612819"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph251617564281">获取UB空间的大小，单位为byte。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row34503263319">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40.089999999999996%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p543714258341"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00190.html">GetRuntimeUBSize</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.91%" headers="mcps1.3.5.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9464325332"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph11371104916159">获取运行时UB空间的大小，单位为byte。</span>开发者根据UB的大小来计算循环次数等参数值。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table2017815711517" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表12 </b>Kernel Tiling接口列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11179357358">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" id="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p417975719517">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" id="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1817910573513">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row14180457256">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1518025711512"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0214.html">GET_TILING_DATA</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p818095714515">用于获取算子kernel入口函数传入的tiling信息，并填入注册的Tiling结构体中，此函数会以宏展开的方式进行编译。如果用户注册了多个TilingData结构体，使用该接口返回默认注册的结构体。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row165593514011">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p155603514401"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0215.html">GET_TILING_DATA_WITH_STRUCT</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10569356405">使用该接口指定结构体名称，可获取指定的tiling信息，并填入对应的Tiling结构体中，此函数会以宏展开的方式进行编译。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row146553974019">
