        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p246512399407"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0216.html">GET_TILING_DATA_MEMBER</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18466133917406">用于获取tiling结构体的成员变量。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row818045715513">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1318010571658"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0217.html">TILING_KEY_IS</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p41805570511">在核函数中判断本次执行时的tiling_key是否等于某个key，从而标识tiling_key==key的一条kernel分支。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row930185314421">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1830185311424"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00003.html">REGISTER_TILING_DEFAULT</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p63075319421">用于在kernel侧注册用户使用标准C++语法自定义的默认TilingData结构体。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2047417552427">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1147415594219"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00004.html">REGISTER_TILING_FOR_TILINGKEY</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__zh-cn_topic_0000001526206862_p0824144014589">用于在kernel侧注册与TilingKey相匹配的TilingData自定义结构体；该接口需提供一个逻辑表达式，逻辑表达式以字符串“TILING_KEY_VAR”代指实际TilingKey，表达TIlingKey所满足的范围。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row8142114933417">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p187745415347"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00164.html">REGISTER_NONE_TILING</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1131075619124">在Kernel侧使用标准C++语法自定义的TilingData结构体时，若用户不确定需要注册哪些结构体，可使用该接口告知框架侧需使用未注册的标准C++语法来定义TilingData，并配套<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0215.html">GET_TILING_DATA_WITH_STRUCT</a>，<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0216.html">GET_TILING_DATA_MEMBER</a>，<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00140.html">GET_TILING_DATA_PTR_WITH_STRUCT</a>来获取对应的TilingData。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row131802571959">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p51807572055"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0218.html">KERNEL_TASK_TYPE_DEFAULT</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1618045718520">设置全局默认的kernel type，对所有的tiling key生效。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row21801457154">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="39.900000000000006%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0180185713514"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0218.html">KERNEL_TASK_TYPE</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60.099999999999994%" headers="mcps1.3.5.12.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p201806571756">设置某一个具体的tiling key对应的kernel type。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table19526741203211" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表13 </b>ISASI接口列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row352624118322">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" id="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p88065174816">分类</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" id="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14526241173218">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" id="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p145261141203210">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1216412458149">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="2" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p259453711267">标量计算</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5421318131512"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00089.html">WriteGmByPassDCache</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p204221851520"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph94221821518">不经过DCache向GM地址上写数据。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row69501477146">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p74217189157"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00090.html">ReadGmByPassDCache</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19422018191516"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph14211188156">不经过DCache从GM地址上读数据。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row154401144114718">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="15" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12441154416479">矢量计算</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20441544114710"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0221.html">VectorPadding</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p244134434718">根据padMode（pad模式）与padSide（pad方向）对源操作数按照datablock进行填充操作。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row10526441173211">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1994405814488"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0222.html">BilinearInterpolation</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1526144123212">双线性插值操作，分为垂直迭代和水平迭代。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1952734143214">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p99449586481"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0223.html">GetCmpMask</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1152720414327">获取<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0067.html">Compare（结果存入寄存器）</a>指令的比较结果。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row946564134915">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5465104110496"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0224.html">SetCmpMask</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p184655418496">为<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0070.html">Select</a>不传入mask参数的接口设置比较寄存器。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1952774119326">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8429183014919"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0225.html">GetReduceRepeatSumSpr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p165272418326">获取<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0078.html">ReduceSum</a>（针对tensor前n个数据计算）接口的计算结果。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row125271041103210">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12638205318500"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0226.html">GetReduceRepeatMaxMinSpr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p195452267225">获取<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0076.html">ReduceMax</a>、<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0077.html">ReduceMin</a>连续场景下的最大/最小值以及相应的索引值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row185271941123212">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p169441258134818"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0227.html">ProposalConcat</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14527241173218">将连续元素合入Region Proposal内对应位置，每次迭代会将16个连续元素合入到16个Region Proposals的对应位置里。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13527134173211">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p39441158184816"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0228.html">ProposalExtract</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10462291478">与ProposalConcat功能相反，从Region Proposals内将相应位置的单个元素抽取后重排，每次迭代处理16个Region Proposals，抽取16个元素后连续排列。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row452716417321">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6944165817489"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0229.html">RpSort16</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p773018265170">根据Region Proposals中的score域对其进行排序（score大的排前面），每次排16个Region Proposals。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row745884215480">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p139441358114819"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0230.html">MrgSort4</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p101781237141713">将已经排好序的最多4 条region proposals队列，排列并合并成1条队列，结果按照score域由大到小排序。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1019917563485">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p594585811484"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0231.html">Sort32</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p151991556204813">排序函数，一次迭代可以完成32个数的排序。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row18337125610483">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10945175864811"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0232.html">MrgSort</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p78578171182">将已经排好序的最多4 条队列，合并排列成 1 条队列，结果按照score域由大到小排序。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row8465195684815">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p139456584485"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0233.html">GetMrgSortResult</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p101531828111815">获取<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0232.html">MrgSort</a>或<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0230.html">MrgSort4</a>已经处理过的队列里的Region Proposal个数，并依次存储在四个List入参中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row46098568481">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1794575816483"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0234.html">Gatherb</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4609556174812">给定一个输入的张量和一个地址偏移张量，Gatherb指令根据偏移地址将输入张量收集到结果张量中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row576405615486">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1945158154815"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0235.html">Scatter</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1836365951815">给定一个连续的输入张量和一个目的地址偏移张量，Scatter指令根据偏移地址生成新的结果张量后将输入张量分散到结果张量中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row55961050102215">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="8" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3596650162219">矢量计算</p>
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p94614512565"></p>
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4570181317114"></p>
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p162501316917"></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1627401015234"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00245.html">Prelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17596550132219"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph171052419253">源操作数src0大于0的情况下直接将src0写入目的操作数dst，否则将源操作数src0 * src1的结果写入dst。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row10490181412234">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p768371913238"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00246.html">Mull</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17491151412313"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph711422918258">对前count个输入数据src0、src1按元素相乘操作，将结果写入dst0Local，溢出部分写入dst1Local。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row610542433612">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17573105103717"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00253.html">AbsSub</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1310512414366"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph4885524194011">将src0Local与src1相减再求绝对值， 并将计算结果写入dst。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row21531226203613">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p968312193716"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00254.html">ExpSub</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15153126143614"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph2080081312419">src0与src1相减，将差值作为指数计算自然常数e的幂次， 并将计算结果写入dst。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row18177628103613">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p128991739174417"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0049.html">MulsCast</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p121781328103612"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph17644543174116">将矢量源操作数前count个数据与标量相乘再按照CAST_ROUND模式转换成half类型， 并将计算结果写入dst，此接口支持标量在前和标量在后两种场景。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11469585616">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p522018118566"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00258.html">Truncate</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p74618519564"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph12508132815572">将源操作数的浮点数元素截断到整数位，同时源操作数的数据类型保持不变。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row195707131411">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1434016281613"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00036.html">Interleave</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p657010139118"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph664518360219">给定源操作数src0和src1，将src0和src1中的元素交织存入结果操作数dst0和dst1中。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19250111610113">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p184274411111"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00037.html">DeInterleave</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p22501166119"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph054752519311">给定源操作数src0和src1，将src0和src1中的元素解交织存入结果操作数dst0和dst1中。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row224111331412">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="2" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p711814301143">数据搬运</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p148821245184915"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0265.html">DataCopyPad</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p019795716484">该接口提供数据非对齐搬运的功能。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row7375515151412">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p148821245194911"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0266.html">SetPadValue</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6689192111598">设置DataCopyPad接口填充的数值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row154051221164915">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="26" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p118921556154813">矩阵计算</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1255212302496"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0249.html">Mmad</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p24861371569">完成矩阵乘加操作。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row12765526165715">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1176511267573"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0250.html">MmadWithSparse</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p47651826105712">完成矩阵乘加操作，传入的左矩阵A为稀疏矩阵， 右矩阵B为稠密矩阵 。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row0435522154918">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8552203094910"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0258.html">SetHF32Mode</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p64161631122120">此接口同<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0259.html">SetHF32TransMode</a>、<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0260.html">SetMMRowMajor</a>以及<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00197.html">SetMMColumnMajor</a>一样，都用于设置寄存器的值。SetHF32Mode接口用于设置MMAD的HF32模式。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1858982214918">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p165526308490"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0259.html">SetHF32TransMode</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1478477185019">此接口同<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0258.html">SetHF32Mode</a>、<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0260.html">SetMMRowMajor</a>以及<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00197.html">SetMMColumnMajor</a>一样，都用于设置寄存器的值。SetHF32TransMode用于设置MMAD的HF32取整模式，仅在MMAD的HF32模式生效时有效。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1374212216499">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19945205916295"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0260.html">SetMMRowMajor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p87421422144917">此接口同<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0258.html">SetHF32Mode</a>、<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0259.html">SetHF32TransMode</a>一样，都用于设置寄存器的值，本接口用于设置MMAD计算时优先通过N方向。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row9217141164613">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14218134119466"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00197.html">SetMMColumnMajor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18317459134619">此接口同<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0258.html">SetHF32Mode</a>、<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0259.html">SetHF32TransMode</a>一样，都用于设置寄存器的值，本接口用于设置MMAD计算时优先通过M方向。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19411232491">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20552113020499"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0262.html">Conv2D</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10261152153414">计算给定输入张量和权重张量的2-D卷积，输出结果张量。Conv2d卷积层多用于图像识别，使用过滤器提取图像中的特征。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row144112573483">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9552930204920"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0263.html">Gemm</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14116577483">根据输入的切分规则，将给定的两个输入张量做矩阵乘，输出至结果张量。将A和B两个输入矩阵乘法在一起，得到一个输出矩阵C。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row147451115151916">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p45523300491"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0252.html">SetFixPipeConfig</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17851154862112"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00130.html">DataCopy</a>（CO1-&gt;GM、CO1-&gt;A1）过程中进行随路量化时，通过调用该接口设置量化流程中tensor量化参数。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row294174016209">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p65522030174916"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0253.html">SetFixpipeNz2ndFlag</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2898124417263"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00130.html">DataCopy</a>（CO1-&gt;GM、CO1-&gt;A1）过程中进行随路格式转换（NZ2ND）时，通过调用该接口设置NZ2ND相关配置。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11129104162017">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p165521930144913"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0254.html">SetFixpipePreQuantFlag</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16332111515234"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00130.html">DataCopy</a>（CO1-&gt;GM、CO1-&gt;A1）过程中进行随路量化时，通过调用该接口设置量化流程中scalar量化参数。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19331114117200">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p278152219274"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0255.html">SetFixPipeClipRelu</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10787226279"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00130.html">DataCopy</a>（CO1-&gt;GM）过程中进行随路量化后，通过调用该接口设置ClipRelu操作的最大值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row0541441152019">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1674634112715"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0256.html">SetFixPipeAddr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p71911024132712"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00130.html">DataCopy</a>（CO1-&gt;GM）过程中进行随路量化后，通过调用该接口设置element-wise操作时LocalTensor的地址。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row08411461812">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1955143017499"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0237.html">Fill</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1489215634815">初始化LocalTensor（TPosition为A1/A2/B1/B2）为某一个具体的数值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row696114470313">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p455133074917"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0238.html">LoadData</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3448142813492">LoadData包括Load2D和Load3D数据加载功能。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row8181746635">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20551143074919"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0239.html">LoadDataWithTranspose</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p124281245667">该接口实现带转置的2D格式数据从A1/B1到A2/B2的加载。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row194791339636">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12211416165318"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0240.html">SetAippFunctions</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p192129164538">设置图片预处理（AIPP，AI core pre-process）相关参数。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1948463715319">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1061103015541"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0241.html">LoadImageToLocal</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p792614613548">将图像数据从GM搬运到A1/B1。 搬运过程中可以完成图像预处理操作：包括图像翻转，改变图像尺寸（抠图，裁边，缩放，伸展），以及色域转换，类型转换等。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row0248535339">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16995181110545"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0242.html">LoadUnZipIndex</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20995111113541">加载GM上的压缩索引表到内部寄存器。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row132838331033">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1434981445411"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0243.html">LoadDataUnzip</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2349121415547">将GM上的数据解压并搬运到A1/B1/B2上。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row7838730733">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1418115368566"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0244.html">LoadDataWithSparse</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7263112805617">用于搬运存放在B1里的512B的稠密权重矩阵到B2里，同时读取128B的索引矩阵用于稠密矩阵的稀疏化。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row66591928736">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1552163012493"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0245.html">SetFmatrix</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1365632015497">用于调用<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0238.html">Load3Dv1/Load3Dv2</a>时设置FeatureMap的属性描述。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row07692515218">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p055233018498"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0246.html">SetLoadDataBoundary</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p37801058115614">设置<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0238.html">Load3D</a>时A1/B1边界值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row3787671224">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1155273074915"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0247.html">SetLoadDataRepeat</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p532619442559">用于设置Load3Dv2接口的repeat参数。设置repeat参数后，可以通过调用一次Load3Dv2接口完成多个迭代的数据搬运。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row4662691428">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18552183094913"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0248.html">SetLoadDataPaddingValue</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p53183549554">设置padValue，用于Load3Dv1/Load3Dv2。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row165019119218">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12552123034913"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0251.html">Fixpipe</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7194151162615">矩阵计算完成后，对结果进行处理，例如对计算结果进行量化操作，并把数据从CO1搬迁到Global Memory中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row188711975503">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="5" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13495131445016">同步控制</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14495181465015"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0270.html">SetFlag/WaitFlag</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p158718715503">同一核内不同流水线之间的同步指令。具有数据依赖的不同流水指令之间需要插此同步。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row181116835017">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16495161412505"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0271.html">PipeBarrier</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p21128145011">阻塞相同流水，具有数据依赖的相同流水之间需要插此同步。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row653814222592">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1289326165918"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0272.html">DataSyncBarrier</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8600163212594">用于阻塞后续的指令执行，直到所有之前的内存访问指令（需要等待的内存位置可通过参数控制）执行结束。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row114551284506">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5495141475017"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0273.html">CrossCoreSetFlag</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p20555155242412">针对分离模式，AI Core上的Cube核（AIC）与Vector核（AIV）之间的同步设置指令。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1169128105014">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1495141410509"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0274.html">CrossCoreWaitFlag</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1453712312015">针对分离模式，AI Core上的Cube核（AIC）与Vector核（AIV）之间的同步等待指令。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13905175871">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="3" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p123688281178">同步控制</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p99126441977"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00132.html">Mutex</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1190519517715"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1520973121010">Mutex用于核内异步流水指令之间的同步处理，其功能类似于传统CPU中的锁机制。通过锁定指定流水再释放流水来完成流水间的同步依赖。每个锁有固定的一个MutexID，该ID可通过用户自定义（范围为0-27）或者通过<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00136.html">AllocMutexID/ReleaseMutexID</a>进行申请释放。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row7712137571">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p532817527716"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00136.html">AllocMutexID</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p147126719714">从框架获取并占用一个MutexID，与<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00137.html">ReleaseMutexID</a>配合使用，管理MutexID的获取和释放。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5734121020712">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1989145819720"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00137.html">ReleaseMutexID</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1973571020715"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph10161191691011">从框架释放一个MutexID，与<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00136.html">AllocMutexID</a>配合使用。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row329139125011">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="2" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p164958141507">缓存处理</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p124961914135016"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0276.html">ICachePreLoad</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16327940182218">从指令所在DDR地址预加载指令到ICache中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19196799505">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4496131419504"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0277.html">GetICachePreloadStatus</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p319669165012">获取ICACHE的PreLoad的状态。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2493696509">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="4" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p649661485013">系统变量访问</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1649651485012"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0279.html">GetProgramCounter</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1349315905014">获取程序计数器的指针，程序计数器用于记录当前程序执行的位置。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19631896502">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p74961814175010"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0280.html">GetSubBlockNum</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p966612452211">获取AI Core上Vector核的数量。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row278314916501">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2496314115019"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0281.html">GetSubBlockIdx</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7322151817114">获取AI Core上Vector核的ID。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row591914913509">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p449621416508"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0282.html">GetSystemCycle</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p318519518103">获取当前系统cycle数，若换算成时间需要按照50MHz的频率，时间单位为us，换算公式为：time = (cycle数/50) us 。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row23644391720">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="3" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13856134291819">系统变量访问</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p236104361711"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00091.html">SetCtrlSpr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p123610436177"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1641093112018">对CTRL寄存器（控制寄存器）的特定比特位进行设置。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row191522045131718">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p186893131181"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00092.html">GetCtrlSpr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17152114561719"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1892618377202">读取CTRL寄存器（控制寄存器）特定比特位上的值。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row28763469172">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p876017348182"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00093.html">ResetCtrlSpr</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5876194619170"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph818454202">对CTRL寄存器（控制寄存器）的特定比特位做重置。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row20229161014506">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="4" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p649691414505">原子操作</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p114961614195013"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0284.html">SetAtomicMax</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1555107145615">原子操作函数，设置后续从VECOUT传输到GM的数据是否执行原子比较，将待拷贝的内容和GM已有内容进行比较，将最大值写入GM。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row237920103503">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18496141413508"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0285.html">SetAtomicMin</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15379191018502">原子操作函数，设置后续从VECOUT传输到GM的数据是否执行原子比较，将待拷贝的内容和GM已有内容进行比较，将最小值写入GM。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5515201016501">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4496814165013"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0286.html">SetStoreAtomicConfig</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p95151510125017">设置原子操作使能位与原子操作类型。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row466110104502">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p949614146509"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0287.html">GetStoreAtomicConfig</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4576142854016">获取原子操作使能位与原子操作类型的值。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row161503191126">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1715151915126">调试接口</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9552163084912"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0261.html">CheckLocalMemoryIA</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5863853185711">监视设定范围内的UB读写行为，如果监视到有设定范围的读写行为则会出现EXCEPTION报错，未监视到设定范围的读写行为则不会报错。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row18446920142913">
        <td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="3" valign="top" width="12.379999999999999%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p164464202295">Cube分组管理</p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.63%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p7499133216292"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0289.html">CubeResGroupHandle</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="59.99%" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.3 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p24466202298">CubeResGroupHandle用于在分离模式下通过软同步控制AIC和AIV之间进行通讯，实现<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__zh-cn_topic_0000001588832845_ph168139148536">AI Core</span>计算资源分组。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1493333852918">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8398498294"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0301.html">GroupBarrier</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1493314380295">当同一个<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0289.html">CubeResGroupHandle</a>中的两个AIV任务之间存在依赖关系时，可以使用GroupBarrier控制同步。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1786094112299">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p186215598290"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0307.html">KfcWorkspace</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.3.5.13.2.4.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1776114192019">KfcWorkspace为通信空间描述符，管理不同<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0289.html">CubeResGroupHandle</a>的消息通信区划分，与CubeResGroupHandle配合使用。KfcWorkspace的构造函数用于创建KfcWorkspace对象。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
   </div>

       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1523406156">

         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1952380121514">用于对SoftMax相关计算结果做后处理，调整SoftMax的计算结果为指定的值。</p></td>

       </tr>

   <div class="section" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__section1416490133020">
    <h4 class="sectiontitle">SIMT API</h4>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table355621172410" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表27 </b>核函数定义API
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row105561111192410">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" id="mcps1.3.7.2.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p11556191111244">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" id="mcps1.3.7.2.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1655618115241">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row10556171192410">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12556111118244"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10303.html">asc_vf_call</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1179785312220">启动SIMT VF（Vector Function）子任务，启动指定数目的线程，执行指定的SIMT核函数。</p></td>
       </tr>

    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table1990955453318" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表28 </b>同步函数
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row690975463312">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" id="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p189091354173316">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" id="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15909154153315">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row209091454163314">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p79091954113318"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10306.html">asc_syncthreads</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1690965443315">等待当前thread block内所有thread代码都执行到该函数位置。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1390945433317">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1590925411337"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10307.html">asc_threadfence</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p139091542339">用于保证不同核对同一份全局、共享内存的访问过程中，写入操作的时序性。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1415719381045">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p101577387413"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10449.html">asc_threadfence_block</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14158138445">用于协调同一线程块（Thread Block）内线程之间的内存操作顺序，确保某一线程在调用asc_threadfence_block()之前的所有内存读写操作对同一线程块内的其他线程可见。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table139091354143312" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">

      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row139091547334">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" id="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1090916540331">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" id="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15909154183312">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1190910544330">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13916125433311">获取输入数据的差值，差值小于0时，返回0。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row119165548334">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12916195415332"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10472.html">remquof</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1791695411333">获取输入数据x除以y的余数。求余数时，商取最接近x除以y浮点数结果的整数，当x除以y的浮点数结果与左右最接近的整数距离相等时，商取偶数，同时将商赋值给指针变量quo。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13916254203313">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14916254133316"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10473.html">fmodf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p491615453316">获取输入数据x除以y的余数。求余数时，商取x除以y浮点数结果的整数部分。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1791614546337">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p139161541336"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10474.html">remainderf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5916185418337">获取输入数据x除以y的余数。求余数时，商取最接近x除以y浮点数结果的整数，当x除以y的浮点数结果与左右最接近的整数距离相等时，商取偶数。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row091635423313">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p209161154133315">获取由第一个输入x的数值部分和第二个输入y的符号部分拼接得到的浮点数。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row12916105473310">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1091665433317"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10476.html">nearbyIntf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16917354163318">获取与输入浮点数最接近的整数，输入浮点数与左右整数的距离相等时，返回偶数。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row129171654113313">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p191775483318"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10477.html">nextafterf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18917185418334">如果y大于x，返回比x大的下一个可表示的浮点值，即浮点数二进制最低位加1。</p>
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8917354133310">如果y小于x，返回比x小的下一个可表示的浮点值，即浮点数二进制最低位减1。</p>
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1991745433314">如果y等于x，返回x。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row149171154153319">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.4.2.3.1.1 ">

       </tr>

       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row16744631793">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p874412316911"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10416.html">__hisnan</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15744113114918">判断浮点数是否为nan。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5396191822512">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p93961918152514"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10417.html">isinf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18215105443017">判断浮点数是否为无穷。</p></td>

         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p199724331919"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10418.html">__hisinf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.6.2.3.1.2 ">

    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table17209165495117" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表32 </b>Atomic函数
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row720915541514">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" id="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16210954205119">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" id="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p122101254105114">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row221025405119">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18210165415117"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10375.html">asc_atomic_add</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14210105415119">对<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph10536132505718">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1753616252577">Global Memory</span>上的数据与指定数据执行原子加操作，即将指定数据累加到<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph15143152082811">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph214322082812">Global Memory</span>的数据中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row102101054135111">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10210654105113"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10376.html">asc_atomic_sub</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1021019548518">对<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph5434440195718">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1343414075710">Global Memory</span>上的数据与指定数据执行原子减操作，即在<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph93051920194612">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1330532015466">Global Memory</span>的数据上减去指定数据。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row14210155416511">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p12101954125120"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10377.html">asc_atomic_exch</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p921017541514">对<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph19824155135715">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph2824251175717">Global Memory</span>地址做原子赋值操作，即将指定数据赋值到<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph38242515577">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1782418514572">Global Memory</span>地址中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row9210125445116">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16210654145112"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10378.html">asc_atomic_max</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p182101754115113">对<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph82259014584">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph722514018587">Global Memory</span>数据做原子求最大值操作，即将<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph72255035818">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph3225180185819">Global Memory</span>的数据与指定数据中的最大值赋值到<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph16444335125614">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph2444133513562">Global Memory</span>地址中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row5210165425115">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p321035415115"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10379.html">asc_atomic_min</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16210155445111">对<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph236151245817">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph17361012165811">Global Memory</span>数据做原子求最小值操作，即将<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph103671216583">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph43616124587">Global Memory</span>的数据与指定数据中的最小值赋值到<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1336191245816">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1236121265812">Global Memory</span>地址中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row162101254175110">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p421011547518"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10380.html">asc_atomic_inc</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0210115417511">对<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph4991141914580">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph18991719175819">Global Memory</span>上address的数值进行原子加1操作，如果address上的数值大于等于指定数值val，则对address赋值为0，否则将address上数值加1。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19210145435112">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2210115455112"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10381.html">asc_atomic_dec</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1021025445117">对<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1959983155818">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph05991831195817">Global Memory</span>上address的数值进行原子减1操作，如果address上的数值等于0或大于指定数值val，则对address赋值为val，否则将address上数值减1。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13198193011527">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p48379327525"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10382.html">asc_atomic_cas</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p101941330125214">对<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph8959114015812">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph0959144013587">Global Memory</span>上address的数值进行原子比较赋值操作，如果address上的数值等于指定数值compare，则对address赋值为指定数值val，否则address的数值不变。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1819812308522">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18837203213524"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10383.html">asc_atomic_and</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p919417304529">对<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph18404486584">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph138406481583">Global Memory</span>上address的数值与指定数值val进行原子与（&amp;）操作，即将address数值与（&amp;）val的结果赋值到<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph7927849219">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph4927742218">Global Memory</span>上。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row11982030105210">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1837163211522"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10384.html">asc_atomic_or</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15194130125218">对<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph652716542215">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1652785412220">Global Memory</span>上address的数值与指定数值val进行原子或（|）操作，即将address数值或（|）val的结果赋值到<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1333582155916">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph13335112125912">Global Memory</span>上。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row819816309523">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p108358323529"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10385.html">asc_atomic_xor</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p8194203065216">对<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph12461111115918">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph19461181110597">Global Memory</span>上address的数值与指定数值val进行原子异或（^）操作，即将address数值异或（^）val的结果赋值到<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph746121145910">Unified Buffer</span>或<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1946111113591">Global Memory</span>上。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>

       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row937475185312">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1 ">

       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row16374452533">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p737413565311"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10391.html">asc_shfl</a></p></td>

      <caption>
       <b>表34 </b>类型转换
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row143991736144719">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="50%" id="mcps1.3.7.9.2.3.1.1">

         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1135344418116">从两个bfloat16_t类型数据创建bfloat16x2_t类型的向量。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table17209142275014" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表36 </b>使能Cache Hints的Load/Store函数
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row120922211507">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" id="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1220962210501">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" id="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p620912295018">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row180373015523">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p68031330105213"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10556.html">asc_ldcg</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2094931913815">从L2 Cache加载缓存的数据，如果缓存命中，则直接返回数据。若未命中，则从<span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph4433133645514">Global Memory</span>地址预加载数据缓存至L2 Cache，并返回数据。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row185241328175218">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1352432816526"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10557.html">asc_ldca</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p95231912151116">首先从Data Cache加载缓存数据，若未命中，则尝试从L2 Cache加载。如果Data Cache和L2 Cache中均未找到所需数据，则从Global Memory中读取数据，然后将其缓存到L2 Cache和Data Cache中。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row83103233528">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p13310182315219"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10558.html">asc_stcg</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10735143121114">将指定数据存储到Global Memory的地址address中，并缓存到L2 Cache，但不缓存至Data Cache。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row142096228500">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="40%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2209172255013"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10559.html">asc_stwt</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="60%" headers="mcps1.3.7.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19524191351212">将指定数据存储到Global Memory的地址address中，并缓存至Data Cache和L2 Cache。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
   </div>

       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row99214327228">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.8.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5921163211227"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10446.html">add_lvalue_reference</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.8.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p492143211222">在程序编译时，为指定类型添加左值引用限定符。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row4302033132213">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.8.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p3302163352214"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10447.html">add_rvalue_reference</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.8.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p630218339222">在程序编译时，为指定类型添加右值引用限定符。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row498311541816">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.8.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p4286105318107"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10117.html">enable_if</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.8.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1328665317104">在程序编译时根据某个条件启用或禁用特定的函数模板、类模板或模板特化。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row49836541682">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.8.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18285105321019"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10118.html">conditional</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.8.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1928525319102">在程序编译时根据一个布尔条件从两个类型中选择一个类型。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15509255143618">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.71%" headers="mcps1.3.8.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p968185918363"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10198.html">integral_constant</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.29%" headers="mcps1.3.8.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15098558367">用于封装一个编译时常量整数值，是标准库中许多类型特性和编译时计算的基础组件。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table32991747162610" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表38 </b>平台信息获取API列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row13299184712616">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.6%" id="mcps1.3.8.3.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p15299104762613">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.4%" id="mcps1.3.8.3.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p123001847142611">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row63002047192611">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.6%" headers="mcps1.3.8.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p530034762613"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1026.html">PlatformAscendC</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.4%" headers="mcps1.3.8.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p43001947152612">在实现Host侧的Tiling函数时，可能需要获取一些硬件平台的信息，来支撑Tiling的计算，比如获取硬件平台的核数等信息。PlatformAscendC类提供获取这些平台信息的功能。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row6300134719269">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.6%" headers="mcps1.3.8.3.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14300184720265"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1039.html">PlatformAscendCManager</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.4%" headers="mcps1.3.8.3.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1330019473269">基于Kernel Launch算子工程，通过基础调用（Kernel Launch）方式调用算子的场景下，可能需要获取硬件平台相关信息，比如获取硬件平台的核数。PlatformAscendCManager类提供获取平台信息的功能。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">

      <thead align="left">

       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19311619195">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.55%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9931615198"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0945.html">原型注册接口（OP_ADD）</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p189320611195">注册算子的原型定义。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row169320619198">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.55%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.1 ">

         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p14936612191">用于算子原型定义。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row16935614198">

        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18938611917">用于算子参数定义。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19386121911">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.55%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p79315661915"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0976.html">OpAttrDef</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p129315619195">用于算子属性定义。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row169415613197">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.55%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1294106171920"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0978.html">OpAICoreDef</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1594116181913">用于定义AI处理器上相关实现信息，并关联Tiling实现、Shape推导等函数。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row2949661912">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.55%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1194166101912"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0988.html">OpAICoreConfig</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19947620190">用于配置AI Core配置信息。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row10946641913">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.55%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1994186111919"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_0999.html">OpMC2Def</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" headers="mcps1.3.8.4.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p69420601919">该类用于在host侧配置通算融合算子的通信域名称。配置后在kernel侧可以获取通信域对应的context地址。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
