     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table1518794581918" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表40 </b>Tiling数据结构注册API列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row21887458194">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.55%" id="mcps1.3.8.5.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p101881545171917">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" id="mcps1.3.8.5.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1218824531915">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1319034531918">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.55%" headers="mcps1.3.8.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p10190245111913"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1005.html">TilingData结构定义</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" headers="mcps1.3.8.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p619064571919">定义一个TilingData的类，添加所需的成员变量（TilingData字段），用于保存所需TilingData参数。完成该TilingData类的定义后，该类通过继承TilingDef类（用来存放、处理用户自定义Tiling结构体成员变量的基类）提供TilingData字段设置、序列化和保存等接口。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row619054501914">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.55%" headers="mcps1.3.8.5.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1219044520192"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1006.html">TilingData结构注册</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.45%" headers="mcps1.3.8.5.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1019034511196">注册定义的TilingData结构体并和自定义算子绑定。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table2675125415261" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">

      </caption>
      <thead align="left">

        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" id="mcps1.3.8.6.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p367695412260">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" id="mcps1.3.8.6.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p0676145422615">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1010110512216">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2010212532219"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00071.html">OpTilingRegistry</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" headers="mcps1.3.8.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p710211522210"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph134802046173619">OpTilingRegistry类属于context_ascendc命名空间，主要用于加载Tiling实现的动态库，并获取算子的Tiling函数指针以进行调试和验证。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1867785432614">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.6.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p967795492618"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_1007.html">ContextBuilder</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" headers="mcps1.3.8.6.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p56781254122619">ContextBuilder类提供一系列的API接口，支持手动构造<a href="/document/detail/zh/CANNCommunityEdition/900/maintenref/basicdataapi/atlasopapi_07_00223.html">TilingContext</a>类来验证Tiling函数以及KernelContext类用于TilingParse函数的验证。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table2864441102011" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表42 </b>Tiling模板编程API列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row4832193752110">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.44%" id="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p157001846132117">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.56%" id="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1570016468218">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1486515413204">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.44%" headers="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p111901452192"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00011.html">模板参数定义</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.56%" headers="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5191124551911">通过该类接口进行模板参数ASCENDC_TPL_ARGS_DECL和模板参数组合ASCENDC_TPL_ARGS_SEL（即可使用的模板）的定义。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row198667417207">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.44%" headers="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p17191145131910"><a href="atlas_ascendc_10_00008.html">GET_TPL_TILING_KEY</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.56%" headers="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1619174520193">Tiling模板编程时，开发者通过调用此接口自动生成TilingKey。该接口将传入的模板参数通过定义的位宽，转成二进制，按照顺序组合后转成uint64数值，即TilingKey。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row586654192019">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.44%" headers="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p419110458198"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00057.html">ASCENDC_TPL_SEL_PARAM</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.56%" headers="mcps1.3.8.7.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p819119458193"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1519110455190">Tiling模板编程时，开发者通过调用此接口自动生成并配置TilingKey。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table2173036112514" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">

      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row161714011250">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.3%" id="mcps1.3.8.8.2.3.1.1">

        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.7%" id="mcps1.3.8.8.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p26609019267">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row917323610256">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.3%" headers="mcps1.3.8.8.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1419164561913"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00060.html">DEVICE_IMPL_OP_OPTILING</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.7%" headers="mcps1.3.8.8.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p9191945151920"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph419114452199">在<a href="/document/detail/zh/CANNCommunityEdition/900/programug/Ascendcopdevg/atlas_ascendc_10_00014.html">Tiling下沉</a>场景中，该宏定义用于生成Tiling下沉的注册类，再通过调用注册类的成员函数来注册需要下沉的Tiling函数。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>

      </caption>
      <thead align="left">

        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" id="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.2">

      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1390513568263">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p5905156122619"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00154.html">aclrtcCompileProg</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p149052056172612"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph17905125672617">编译接口，编译指定的程序。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row3905185632610">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p99051356182611"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00155.html">aclrtcCreateProg</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p6905205652615"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph8905456152619">通过给定的参数，创建编译程序的实例。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row15905105615268">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p190625642617"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00156.html">aclrtcDestroyProg</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1490685613261"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph169061356152611">销毁编译程序的实例。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row8906956122613">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p139061156142610"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00157.html">aclrtcGetBinData</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p590617568261"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph5906145618261">获取编译后的二进制数据。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row790685613266">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p18906185642615"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00158.html">aclrtcGetBinDataSize</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2906135614269"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph190617563264">获取编译的二进制数据大小。用于在<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00157.html">aclrtcGetBinData</a>获取二进制数据时分配对应大小的内存空间。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row29061056112617">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p390635610265"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00159.html">aclrtcGetCompileLogSize</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19906165612617"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1290635618262">获取编译日志的大小。用于在<a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00160.html">aclrtcGetCompileLog</a>获取日志内容时分配对应大小的内存空间。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row9907185610267">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p16907105652615"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00160.html">aclrtcGetCompileLog</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" headers="mcps1.3.8.9.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p149072056172614"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph19907156142610">获取编译日志的内容，以字符串形式保存。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table1514223372716" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表45 </b>log API列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row3142033112715">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.79%" id="mcps1.3.8.10.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p181421633182714">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.21%" id="mcps1.3.8.10.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1114213332712">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row914623311278">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.79%" headers="mcps1.3.8.10.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p014643382720"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00152.html">ASC_CPU_LOG</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.21%" headers="mcps1.3.8.10.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p101461833162715"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1014643313273">提供Host侧打印Log的功能。开发者可以在算子的TilingFunc代码中使用ASC_CPU_LOG_XXX接口来输出相关内容。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
    <div class="tablenoborder">
     <table cellpadding="4" cellspacing="0" summary="" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__table17826192512135" frame="border" border="1" rules="all">
      <caption>
       <b>表46 </b>调测接口列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row10826162551319">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.79%" id="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p782622512134">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.21%" id="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p982712512136">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row48271425151314">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.79%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p128276255138"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10426.html">printf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.21%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p182718253134"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph1565753361414">在算子Kernel侧的实现代码中，需要输出日志信息时，调用printf接口打印相关内容。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1597951912417">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.79%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p397971912416"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10428.html">assert</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.21%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p697901913247">本接口在SIMT VF调试场景下提供assert断言功能。在算子Kernel侧的SIMT VF实现代码中，如果assert的内部条件判断不为真，则会输出assert条件，并将输入的信息格式化打印在屏幕上。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row497811224246">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.79%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19781122132416"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10429.html">__trap</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.21%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1297852218244">在SIMT VF实现代码中调用此接口会中断算子的运行。</p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1765213536149">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.79%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p19652105310140"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_10448.html">clock</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.21%" headers="mcps1.3.8.11.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p277505112411">本接口在SIMT VF调试场景中提供Clock时间戳功能，用于记录从程序启动到接口调用时刻所经历的时钟周期数（Cycle Count），便于精确分析执行延迟和性能瓶颈。</p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
   </div>
   <div class="section" id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__section06362251213">
    <h4 class="sectiontitle">AI CPU API</h4>
    <div class="tablenoborder">

      <caption>
       <b>表47 </b>AI CPU API列表
      </caption>
      <thead align="left">
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row1440344222113">
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" id="mcps1.3.9.2.2.3.1.1">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p140384218217">接口名</p></th>
        <th align="left" class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" id="mcps1.3.9.2.2.3.1.2">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1140384218214">功能描述</p></th>
       </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row19403174252116">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.9.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p2040314420218"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00166.html">printf</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" headers="mcps1.3.9.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p340494292115"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph15404164211219">该接口提供AI CPU算子Kernel调试场景下的格式化输出功能，默认将输出内容解析并打印在屏幕上。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
       <tr id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__row10404124212210">
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="37.419999999999995%" headers="mcps1.3.9.2.2.3.1.1 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p129831814226"><a href="atlasascendc_api_07_00167.html">assert</a></p></td>
        <td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="62.580000000000005%" headers="mcps1.3.9.2.2.3.1.2 ">
         <p id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__p1404204252114"><span id="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002531382050__ph040464220211">该接口实现AI CPU算子Kernel调试场景下的assert断言功能。</span></p></td>
       </tr>
      </tbody>
     </table>
    </div>
